snd\+\_\+pcm\+\_\+group\+\_\+for\+\_\+each\+\_\+entry -\/ iterate over the linked substreams @s\+: the iterator @substream\+: the substream

Iterate over the all linked substreams to the given @substream. When @substream isn\textquotesingle{}t linked with any others, this gives returns @substream itself once. \mbox{\Hypertarget{pcm_8h_a2fd1e958488e8168c9a34c17c22ff391}\label{pcm_8h_a2fd1e958488e8168c9a34c17c22ff391}}

snd\+\_\+pcm\+\_\+stream\+\_\+lock\+\_\+irqsave -\/ Lock the P\+CM stream @substream\+: P\+CM substream @flags\+: irq flags

This locks the P\+CM stream like \mbox{\hyperlink{pcm_8h_ad73a508b6fd3935e1f565efa7068f08f}{snd\+\_\+pcm\+\_\+stream\+\_\+lock()}} but with the local I\+RQ (only when nonatomic is false). snd\+\_\+pcm\+\_\+lib\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+vmalloc\+\_\+32\+\_\+buffer -\/ allocate 32-\/bit-\/addressable buffer @substream\+: the substream to allocate the buffer to @size\+: the requested buffer size, in bytes

This function works like \mbox{\hyperlink{pcm_8h_ae874fabec1a37632fe4d6437315a42e3}{snd\+\_\+pcm\+\_\+lib\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+vmalloc\+\_\+buffer()}}, but uses \mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2vmalloc_8h_ab13f9a0005487cfe0758bf85e071c9cb}{vmalloc\+\_\+32()}}, i.\+e., the pages are allocated from 32-\/bit-\/addressable memory.

Return\+: 1 if the buffer was changed, 0 if not changed, or a negative error code. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
% FIG 29

snd\+\_\+pcm\+\_\+lib\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+vmalloc\+\_\+buffer -\/ allocate virtual D\+MA buffer @substream\+: the substream to allocate the buffer to @size\+: the requested buffer size, in bytes

Allocates the P\+CM substream buffer using \mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2vmalloc_8h_a5e7539e7696efb631504a768037b6752}{vmalloc()}}, i.\+e., the memory is contiguous in kernel virtual space, but not in physical memory. Use this if the buffer is accessed by kernel code but not by device D\+MA.

Return\+: 1 if the buffer was changed, 0 if not changed, or a negative error code. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
% FIG 30
\mbox{\Hypertarget{pcm_8h_a2d44626d50347eddf7b415c1d1ceb2bb}\label{pcm_8h_a2d44626d50347eddf7b415c1d1ceb2bb}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily static void snd\+\_\+pcm\+\_\+trigger\+\_\+done (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsnd__pcm__substream}{snd\+\_\+pcm\+\_\+substream}} $\ast$}]{substream,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsnd__pcm__substream}{snd\+\_\+pcm\+\_\+substream}} $\ast$}]{master }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

snd\+\_\+pcm\+\_\+trigger\+\_\+done -\/ Mark the master substream @substream\+: the pcm substream instance @master\+: the linked master substream

When multiple substreams of the same card are linked and the hardware supports the single-\/shot operation, the driver calls this in the loop in \mbox{\hyperlink{pcm_8h_aa97f95436c3711e077a427c2bbbbece5}{snd\+\_\+pcm\+\_\+group\+\_\+for\+\_\+each\+\_\+entry()}} for marking the substream as \char`\"{}done\char`\"{}. Then most of trigger operations are performed only to the given master substream.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} trigger\+\_\+master mark is cleared at timestamp updates at the end of trigger operations. \mbox{\Hypertarget{pcm_8h_aa0901992e00b4e362bb1a3bdabe720b8}\label{pcm_8h_aa0901992e00b4e362bb1a3bdabe720b8}}
